# Corpus coverage and source-by-source costs

Generated by `python3 tools/coverage_report.py` from the complete registered metadata export and saved source inventories. Every requested source row is retained; a proxy association is explicitly labeled and is never a port of the named protocol. WU uses the in-tree runner. The overlay column is always **ESTIMATE**, even when the original expression cost is measured.

## Coverage and freshness

Registered entries: **151**. Counting groups after explicit variant merges: **146**. Full bounded spend/verifier groups: **104**. The raw gap to150 is 0; the conservative distinct-group gap is **4**. Neither backlog rows nor source protocols count as programs.

Classification means complete within the stated leaf/verifier interface and bounds, not a complete deployed protocol or a consensus safety audit. Kernels and protocol components remain in the inventory but do not masquerade as full applications. The exact/native CTV implementations, CSFS and HTLC variants, two P2MR constructions, and SHRINCS/SLH-DSA stateless reference profiles each share their corresponding group.

## Every registered program


Test-qualified goal count: 0 groups credited from this stale runner snapshot; no historical pass is credited to changed source. Current vector-only diagnostic passes remain separate evidence, linked in the witness-binding audit.
The full-scope classification above describes the implemented interface; it does not certify a verifier whose current vectors have not passed.

All old runner costs are historical. Some prior expressions had witness-binding or canonical-encoding defects; their passing honest vectors did not establish a correct verifier. These costs must not be attributed to the repaired programs. Current vector-only evidence is recorded separately in [WITNESS-BINDING-AUDIT.md](WITNESS-BINDING-AUDIT.md).

## Every ranked protocol and execution-source row

TVL includes BOTH complete saved top20 sets for each chain: all categories including custodial holdings, and the separately filtered non-CEX ranking. Their source_set values are distinct; overlapping protocols are not merged or counted as additional applications. Gas and compute tables cover **all rows** of the complete one-block source TSVs, including unknown implementations and non-contract recipients. They do not replace unavailable Etherscan/Solscan period rankings. Source period/coverage limits remain those documented in [contract-inventory.md](contract-inventory.md). Architectural PROXY costs belong to the named corpus programs, not to the source contract itself.

The separate [Soltop period supplement](PERIOD-RANKINGS.md) adds all 20 provider-reported last-24h rows captured on 2026-11-05 at 08:23:43 UTC. Its exact ranking cutoffs, ingestion completeness, nested-CPI and failed-transaction accounting are unknown; do not call these exclusive CU or combine them numerically with the one-slot exclusive-CU sample. This is not a recovered Solscan top100 or Ethereum period ranking. All four provider metrics retain full source precision below. Unknown implementations remain explicit gaps.

The [Ethereum UTC day supplement](ETHEREUM-DAY-GAS-RANKING.md) reconciles every receipt in all 7,176 blocks on 2026-11-04. This section shows its entire verified top 20 code-qualified recipient slice, with the [complete 273,361-address rank](../vendor-specs/rankings/ethereum-aws-2026-11-04/all-recipients.tsv.gz) retained separately. Receipt gas is attributed to top-level recipients, including intrinsic and nested execution. The code filter uses the finalized end-of-period block; this is an independent source and metric, not a recovered Etherscan table.

## Growth backlog and counting gate

The conservative gap is 4 additional distinct groups. The backlog below contains 38 prospective new scopes and 34 covered scopes or upgrades that receive no extra counting credit. A source-backed target is not yet an independently validated implementation: full-text/contract review must establish its exact predicate, Bitcoin enforcement path and distinction from existing groups before coding and counting it. If two rows reduce to the same program modulo constants, thresholds or tree depth, merge them. Parameter sweeps remain measurements within one group.

Each accepted new row must supply a full bounded spend predicate or verifier, independent/ upstream vectors including rejection cases, actual jet-substituted WU measurement, and the complete publication/spec provenance. Source-backed candidates whose deployment path depends on unexposed chain context must retain that gap instead of using witness data as an oracle.

| target / count policy | primary specification | distinct work to implement / gate |
| --- | --- | --- |
| lightning-offered-htlc-complete; prospective_new | [BOLT3 offered HTLC](https://github.com/lightning/bolts/blob/master/03-transactions.md) | All offered-HTLC revocation, preimage and timeout signatures with their payout templates; existing two-branch HTLC is a simplification.; Review and pin full specification; define complete bounded Bitcoin enforcement statement; count only if its data flow is distinct from existing groups, not constants or a parameter sweep. |
| ptlc-complete-settlement; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [Schnorr adaptor signatures and DLC specification](https://github.com/discreetlogcontracts/dlcspecs) | Bind the adaptor relation to a complete PTLC payout/refund transaction rather than checking a pre-signature alone.; Covered by registered app-point-time-locked-payment (current vectors/costs still require validation). Current point-lock claim/refund already supplies the bounded payment predicate. Exact off-chain adaptor-session assembly is an upgrade, not another payment application. |
| ark-round-refresh; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [Ark protocol](https://docs.ark-protocol.org/) | Validate a refresh-round input/output commitment and conservation path; unilateral exit and forfeit are already separate leaves.; Registered as app-ark-round-refresh. Current Bark two-leaf refresh and forfeit certificate is complete in client mode. Interactive coordination, chain view and general round sizes remain outside its bound. |
| coinpool-unilateral-exit; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [CoinPool design](https://coinpool.dev/coinpool.pdf) | Full member exit with updated pool key/root, unique payout and amount preservation, not merely cooperative close.; Covered by registered app-payment-pool-merkle-exit (current vectors/costs still require validation). Current authenticated Merkle claim deletion, fixed claimant payout and collateral continuation cover this bounded exit target. Exact CoinPool wire/key construction remains compatibility work. |
| payment-pool-membership-transition; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [TLUV original specification](https://gnusha.org/pi/bitcoindev/20210909065330.GB22496@erisian.com.au/) | Complete pool membership/root update tied to funding input/output values; avoid counting individual TLUV control flags as programs.; Covered by registered app-payment-pool-funded-deposit (current vectors/costs still require validation). Current funded empty-slot insertion authenticates the new pool root and continuing collateral. Exact TLUV control/key semantics are compatibility work, without extra application credit. |
| op-evict-complete; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [OP_EVICT original proposal](https://gnusha.org/pi/bitcoindev/6nZ-SkxvJLrOCOIdUtLOsdnl94DoX_NHY0uwZ7sw78t24FQ33QJlJU95W7Sk1ja5EFic5a3yql14MLmSAYFZvLGBS4lDUJfr8ut9hdB7GD4=@protonmail.com/) | Unique output promises, key subtraction, remaining-party signature and value preservation in one bounded exit predicate.; Covered by registered app-op-evict-complete within its documented bounded native profile. General wire/mode/setup extensions are compatibility work and receive no additional counting credit. |
| numeric-dlc-adaptor-cet; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [DLC specification](https://github.com/discreetlogcontracts/dlcspecs) | Full numeric CET adaptor/oracle composition and payout bounds; current CFD directly verifies an oracle attestation.; Registered as app-numeric-dlc-adaptor-cet. The client verifies one standard-wire numeric CET profile with exact two-digit attestation, adaptor and BIP143 checks. General negotiated, compressed and multi-oracle DLCs are outside it. |
| bolt12-invoice-authorization; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [BOLT12 offer encoding](https://github.com/lightning/bolts/blob/master/12-offer-encoding.md) | Verify the exact TLV Merkle transcript and invoice signature bound to a payment authorization, including unknown fields.; Registered as app-bolt12-invoice-authorization. The client verifies bounded canonical signed invoices, request linkage and unknown TLVs. Payment execution and external reply context remain outside it. |
| silent-payment-output-covenant; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [BIP352 Silent Payments](https://github.com/bitcoin/bips/blob/master/bip-0352.mediawiki) | Complete eligible-input key aggregation, shared-secret/output-key derivation and authenticated target output; missing input provenance must remain explicit.; Registered as app-silent-payment-output-covenant. Exact two-input eligible-key and one-output derivation with authenticated ECDH. This enforces the chosen leaf or signer policy; BIP352 keypath bypass remains possible. |
| bip322-message-control-proof; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [BIP322 Generic Signed Message Format](https://github.com/bitcoin/bips/blob/master/bip-0322.mediawiki) | Full bounded virtual-transaction ownership proof, including exact serialization and signature mode; generic BIP340 alone is not counted again.; Covered by registered app-bip322-message-control-proof within its documented bounded native profile. General wire/mode/setup extensions are compatibility work and receive no additional counting credit. |
| reserve-ownership-and-sum; covered_by_current_no_extra_count | [BIP127 proof of reserves](https://github.com/bitcoin/bips/blob/master/bip-0127.mediawiki) | Authenticate a bounded set of reserve outpoints/values and ownership statements without double counting; reserve-rollover covenant is not this statement.; Registered as app-reserve-ownership-and-sum. The complete two-reserve client proof checks exact value and distinct owned outpoints against externally authenticated UTXOs. Chain membership and solvency remain external. |

## Input provenance

Reproduce after building and successfully running the current full corpus:

```sh
<same-frozen-runner> metadata > reports/data/corpus-metadata.tsv
python3 tools/coverage_report.py --runner <same-frozen-runner>
```

[source-cost-coverage.tsv](data/source-cost-coverage.tsv) is the complete machine-readable source join. No cost is fabricated for an unimplemented or unmapped source.
